A cost-based deployment evaluation (assumed $50/FN, $0.42/FP, 2% error rate) finds an optimal monitor configuration yielding $8.96 per 1000 queries against a $1000 baseline, a 99.1% saving. Optimal composition strategy varies with cost ratio and base rate. The conjunction of stages produces findings no single stage would.

</details>


### [38] [Polite on the Surface, Wrong in Practice: A Curated Dataset for Fixing Honorific Failures in Multilingual Bangla Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22487)
*Md. Asaduzzaman Shuvo,Mahedi Hasan,Md. Tashin Parvez,Azizul Haque Noman,Md. Shafayet Hossain Ovi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了BLADE，一个包含4,196个交互对的文化对齐指令微调数据集和基准框架，用于提升多语言大模型在孟加拉语等低资源场景下的语用能力（结构变体、区域习语、敬语一致性）。通过LoRA+4-bit NF4量化微调DeepSeek-8B和LLaMA-3.2-3B，实验证明在该数据集上微调的模型在结构保真度和敬语对齐方面取得了显著改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM在处理低资源语言（如孟加拉语）的结构变体、区域习语和敬语一致性时存在严重的语用鸿沟，缺乏文化对齐的对话能力。

Method: 构建了BLADE数据集（4,196个精心策划的交互对），并基于该数据集使用LoRA适配器+4-bit NF4量化框架对DeepSeek-8B和LLaMA-3.2-3B进行参数高效微调。

Result: 微调后的模型在结构保真度和敬语对齐方面取得实质性改进，为低资源多语言文本生成的语用差距弥合提供了严格的基准。

Conclusion: BLADE数据集和微调框架有效弥补了MLLM在低资源孟加拉语场景下的语用鸿沟，展示了文化对齐数据在跨语言对话能力提升中的关键作用。

Abstract: Recent advances in Multilingual Large Language Models (MLLMs) have significantly enhanced cross-lingual conversational capabilities, yet modeling culturally nuanced and context-dependent communication remains a critical bottleneck. Specifically, existing state-of-the-art models exhibit a severe pragmatic gap when handling structural variations, regional idioms, and honorific consistencies in low-resource contexts like Bangla. To address this limitation, we introduce a novel, culturally aligned instruction-tuning dataset for \textbf{BangLa Application and DialoguE generation - BLADE} and benchmarking framework comprising $4,196$ meticulously curated interaction pairs. We leverage this resource to systematically fine-tune and evaluate leading open-weight architectures, including DeepSeek-8B and LLaMA-3.2-3B, utilizing parameter-efficient fine-tuning via LoRA adapters in a 4-bit NormalFloat (NF4) quantization framework. Our empirical evaluations demonstrate that models fine-tuned on our dataset yield substantial improvements in structural fidelity and honorific alignment, providing a rigorous benchmark for bridging pragmatic disparities in low-resource multilingual text generation. Code and dataset: https://github.com/ashuvo25/Bangla_Application_LLM/tree/main

</details>


### [39] [BeLink: Biomedical Entity Linking Meets Generative Re-Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22501)
*Darya Shlyk,Stefano Montanelli,Lawrence Hunter*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于指令微调的开源生成模型，将其应用于生物医学实体链接（BEL）管道的重排序阶段，通过集成的set-wise指令微调公式实现了快速准确的候选选择，在多个BEL基准上提升3%-24%的链接准确率并减少推理时间，最终整合到模块化端到端系统BeLink中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大语言模型的生物医学实体链接（BEL）方法计算效率低下，难以在实际场景中部署。

Method: 提出set-wise指令微调公式，对开源生成模型进行指令微调，将其应用于BEL管道的重排序阶段以实现快速准确的候选选择，并整合到模块化端到端系统BeLink中。

Result: 在多个BEL基准上取得了3%-24%的链接准确率提升，同时相比现有最优方法减少了推理时间。

Conclusion: 指令微调开源生成模型在BEL重排序阶段是一种有效且实用的解决方案，兼具高效性和可部署性。

Abstract: Despite recent progress, Biomedical Entity Linking (BEL) with large language models (LLMs) remains computationally inefficient and challenging to deploy in practical settings. In this work, we demonstrate that instruction-tuning of open-source generative models can offer an effective solution when applied at the re-ranking stage of the BEL pipeline. We propose a set-wise instruction-tuning formulation that enables fast and accurate candidate selection. Our method demonstrates strong performance on multiple BEL benchmarks, yielding significant improvements in linking accuracy (3%-24%) while reducing inference time compared to the state-of-the-art. We integrate our generative re-ranker into BeLink, a modular, end-to-end system designed for practical real-world BEL applications.

</details>


### [40] [Scene Abstraction for Lexical Semantics: Structured Representations of Situated Meaning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22542)
*Yejin Cho,Katrin Erk*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Scene Abstraction框架，通过情境场景（事件、实体、环境）和表达轮廓（参与事件、通用属性、诱发情绪）结构化表示词汇的情境化意义，并构建COCA-Scenes数据集，实验表明场景可被人类可靠识别（准确率82.4%），且场景轮廓比ATOMIC替代方案更符合人类对词义的理解（偏好率86.4%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 咖啡和茶虽有诸多相似属性，却能唤起截然不同的情境、氛围和情感联想。词汇意义的这些情境维度是真实且系统的，但在大多数计算词汇表示中仍未被显式建模。

Method: 提出Scene Abstraction框架，构建结构化表示：每个场景包含情境场景（事件、实体、环境）和表达轮廓（参与事件、通用属性、诱发情绪），通过大语言模型的少样本提示实现操作化。构建了包含26个关键词、520个使用实例的COCA-Scenes数据集。

Result: 两个实验表明：场景可被人类可靠识别（准确率82.4%，比纯文本嵌入高11.8个百分点）；场景轮廓在三个语义维度上比基于ATOMIC的替代方案更符合人类对词义的理解（偏好率86.4%）。

Conclusion: 词汇的情境化意义可以通过结构化场景表示进行有效建模，该框架能够捕捉到传统词汇表示中缺失的系统性情境维度。

Abstract: Coffee and tea share many properties, yet they evoke strikingly different situations, atmospheres, and affective associations. These situated dimensions of word meaning are real and systematic, but they remain implicit in most computational representations of lexical meaning. We propose Scene Abstraction, a framework for constructing structured representations of the interpretive scenes that words participate in across usage contexts. Each scene consists of a Contextual Scene (Events, Entities, Setting) and an expression-centered Expression Profile (Engaged events, Generalizable properties, Evoked emotions), operationalized through few-shot prompting of a large language model. Our contributions are three-fold: (1) a structured representation framework for situated lexical meaning; (2) COCA-Scenes, a dataset of 520 usage instances across 26 keywords for distinct scene identification; and (3) empirical evidence from two experiments suggesting that scenes are reliably identifiable across human observers (82.4% accuracy, +11.8 pp over text-only embeddings) and that our scene profiles more closely align with human interpretation of words in context than ATOMIC-based alternatives (86.4% preference across three semantic dimensions).

</details>


### [41] [One prompt is not enough: Instruction Sensitivity Undermines Embedding Model Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22544)
*Yevhen Kostiuk,Kenneth Enevoldsen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 现有指令嵌入模型评估仅使用单条提示，忽略了模型对提示措辞的敏感性。通过对6个模型、11个数据集、990次实验的分析，发现报告分数不能代表提示分布的真实情况，且排行榜排名对提示选择不稳健——任何模型都可以通过选择有利提示升至第一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前最先进的指令嵌入模型虽然普遍采用基于提示的方法，但评估时仅依赖单条提示，忽略了模型对提示措辞的高度敏感性，导致评估结果不可靠。

Method: 对6个嵌入模型、11个数据集、每个数据集15个任务特定提示（共990次实验）进行实证研究，分析提示敏感性对评估结果的影响。

Result: 报告分数不能反映所有合理提示的分数分布；默认提示可能系统性地高估或低估模型性能；通过选择有利提示，任何模型都可以排名第一，排行榜排名对提示选择不稳健。

Conclusion: 单提示评估不足以评估指令调优的嵌入模型，基准测试应纳入提示鲁棒性，要么使用多提示评估，要么在报告点估计的同时报告敏感性指标。

Abstract: Instruction embedding models have become common among state-of-the-art models, however are evaluated using a single prompt per task. The single-point evaluation ignores a main problem of the instruction-based approach namely: sensitivity to the phrasing of the instruction. We present an empirical study of prompt sensitivity across 6 embedding models, 11 datasets, and 15 task-specific prompts per dataset, a total of 990. We show that reported scores misrepresent the distribution of scores over plausible prompts. The default prompt can both systematically understate or overstate performance. Furthermore, we show that the leaderboard ranking is not robust to prompt selection: by choosing prompts favorably, any model in our study can be promoted to first place. Our findings suggest that single-prompt evaluation is insufficient for instruction-tuned embedding models and that benchmarks should incorporate prompt robustness, either by evaluating over multiple prompts or by reporting sensitivity alongside point estimates.

</details>


### [42] [SynAE: A Framework for Measuring the Quality of Synthetic Data for Tool-Calling Agent Evaluations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22564)
*Shuaiqi Wang,Aadyaa Maddi,Zinan Lin,Giulia Fanti*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SynAE是一个用于评估多轮工具调用Agent合成基准测试质量的框架，从指令、工具调用、最终输出和下游评估四个维度衡量合成数据的有效性、保真度和多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际生产环境中的工具调用Agent数据集往往因包含敏感信息或过于稀疏而无法用于测试，因此需要用合成数据替代或增强，但缺乏量化合成数据与真实数据之间关系的评估方法。

Method: 提出SynAE评估框架，包含四个评估类别：(i)任务指令与中间响应、(ii)工具调用、(iii)最终输出、(iv)下游评估，从有效性、保真度和多样性三个角度衡量合成数据质量。

Result: SynAE能够检测合成数据在有效性、保真度和多样性方面的细微变化，并表明单一指标不足以全面表征合成数据质量，需要进行多轴评估。

Conclusion: 多轴评估对于Agent测试中的合成数据质量评估是必要的，SynAE为评估合成基准测试提供了系统化方案。

Abstract: Today, tool-calling agents are commonly evaluated or tested on static datasets of execution traces, including input commands, agent responses, and associated tool calls. However, internal production datasets are often insufficient or unusable for testing; for example, they may contain sensitive or proprietary data, or they may be too sparse to support comprehensive testing (especially pre-deployment). In these settings, practitioners are increasingly replacing or augmenting real datasets with synthetic ones for evaluation purposes. A key challenge is quantifying the relation between these synthetic datasets and the real data. We introduce SynAE, an evaluation framework for assessing how well synthetic benchmarks for multi-turn, tool-calling agents replicate and augment the characteristics of real data trajectories. SynAE assesses the validity, fidelity, and diversity of synthetic data across four metric categories: (i) task instructions and intermediate responses, (ii) tool calls, (iii) final outputs, and (iv) downstream evaluation. We evaluate SynAE using recent agent benchmarks and test common synthetic data failure modes via realistic and controlled generation schemes. SynAE detects fine-grained variations in data validity, fidelity and diversity, and shows that no single metric is sufficient to fully characterize synthetic data quality, motivating a multi-axis evaluation of synthetic data for agent testing. A demo of SynAE is available at https://synae-2026-synae-demo.static.hf.space/index.html, with code at https://github.com/wsqwsq/SynAE.

</details>


### [43] [LANG: Reinforcement Learning for Multilingual Reasoning with Language-Adaptive Hint Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22567)
*Yuchun Fan,Bei Li,Peiguang Li,Yilin Wang,Yongyu Mu,Jian Yang,Xin Chen,Rongxiang Weng,Jingang Wang,Xunliang Cai,Jingbo Zhu,Tong Xiao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出LANG框架，通过语言条件提示引导非英语推理，结合渐进衰减调度和语言自适应切换机制，在提升多语言推理性能的同时保持语言一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习方法在多语言推理场景中面临输入语言一致性与推理质量之间的根本性权衡，无法充分将增强的推理能力迁移到非英语语境。

Method: LANG框架包含两个核心机制：(1) 渐进衰减调度，逐步撤除语言条件提示的支撑；(2) 语言自适应切换，根据特定语言难度调整学习周期。

Result: 在挑战性多语言数学基准上显著提升了推理性能且不损害语言一致性；框架泛化到数学之外的领域，促进跨模型层的语言对齐。

Conclusion: LANG有效解决了多语言推理中语言一致性与推理质量之间的权衡问题，展现了良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning has proven effective for enhancing multi-step reasoning in large language models (LLMs), yet its benefits have not fully translated to multilingual contexts. Existing methods struggle with a fundamental trade-off: prioritizing input-language consistency severely hampers reasoning quality, while prioritizing reasoning often leads to unintended language drift toward English. We address this challenge with LANG, a novel framework that leverages language-conditioned hints to guide exploration in non-English reasoning tasks. Our method incorporates two key mechanisms to prevent dependency on these hints: a progressive decay schedule that gradually withdraws scaffolding, and a language-adaptive switch that tailors learning horizons to specific language difficulties. Empirical results on challenging multilingual mathematical benchmarks reveal that LANG substantially enhances reasoning performance without compromising language consistency. Moreover, we show that our framework generalizes beyond mathematics, fostering more consistent language alignment across model layers

</details>


### [44] [Beyond Temperature: Hyperfitting as a Late-Stage Geometric Expansion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22579)
*Meimingwei Li,Yuanhao Ding,Esteban Garces Arias,Christian Heumann*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文揭示了"超拟合"现象与单纯温度缩放的本质区别，通过对照实验证明其依赖动态、上下文相关的排名重排序机制，而非静态词汇重加权。层级分析发现该效应源于最终Transformer块中特征空间几何扩展（维度增量约+80.8）的"终端扩展"机制，并提出仅更新最后5层的"晚期LoRA"策略以实现高效参数优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解超拟合现象的内在机制，澄清其与简单温度缩放的潜在等价性假说，并验证其是否仅涉及静态词汇重加权。

Method: 采用熵匹配对照实验区分超拟合与温度缩放；通过消融研究验证动态排名重排序机制；进行层级分析定位效应来源；提出Late-Stage LoRA微调策略。

Result: 超拟合与温度缩放本质不同；排除了静态词汇重加权假说；确认了动态上下文依赖的排名重排序机制；定位到最终Transformer块中特征空间几何扩展（约+80.8维）的"终端扩展"效应。

Conclusion: 超拟合通过最终层的特征空间扩展促进尾部token的生成，而非简单的分布锐化；Late-Stage LoRA能以极小参数更新实现稳健生成。

Abstract: Recent work has identified a counterintuitive phenomenon termed "Hyperfitting", where fine-tuning Large Language Models (LLMs) to near-zero training loss on small datasets surprisingly enhances open-ended generation quality and mitigates repetition in greedy decoding. While effective, the underlying mechanism remains poorly understood, with the extremely low-entropy output distributions suggesting a potential equivalence to simple temperature scaling. In this work, we demonstrate that this phenomenon is fundamentally distinct from distribution sharpening; entropy-matched control experiments reveal that temperature scaling fails to replicate the diversity gains of hyperfitting. Furthermore, we falsify the hypothesis of static vocabulary reweighting, showing through ablation studies that hyperfitting relies on a dynamic, context-dependent rank reordering mechanism. Layer-wise analysis localizes this effect to a "Terminal Expansion" in the final transformer block, where a substantial geometric expansion of the feature space (Delta Dim approx +80.8) facilitates the promotion of deep-tail tokens. Additionally, we introduce Late-Stage LoRA, a targeted fine-tuning strategy that updates only the final 5 layers, yielding robust generation with minimal parameter updates

</details>


### [45] [Agentic CLEAR: Automating Multi-Level Evaluation of LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22608)
*Asaf Yehudai,Lilach Eden,Michal Shmueli-Scheuer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Agentic CLEAR是一个自动、动态、易用的智能体评估框架，能在系统、轨迹、节点三个粒度生成文本洞察，与人工标注错误高度对齐，并能预测任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体评估工具局限于可观测性和基本评估能力，或使用静态的手工错误分类法，无法适应新领域。随着智能体系统自主性增强，迫切需要自动、动态且易用的评估框架。

Method: 提出Agentic CLEAR框架，运行在可观测层之上，支持无缝集成；在系统、轨迹和节点三个粒度生成文本洞察；配备直观UI，使评估高度可访问。

Result: 在四个基准、七种智能体设置和数万次LLM调用上验证，Agentic CLEAR能生成高质量、数据驱动的反馈，与人工标注错误高度对齐，并能预测任务成功率。

Conclusion: Agentic CLEAR提供了自动、动态、多粒度的智能体行为评估方案，弥补了现有工具在适应性和洞察力方面的不足。

Abstract: Agentic systems are becoming more capable: agents define strategies, take actions, and interact with different environments. This autonomy poses serious challenges for overseeing and assessing agent behavior. Most current tools are limited, focusing on observability with basic evaluation capabilities or imposing static, hand-crafted error taxonomies that cannot adapt to new domains. To address this gap, we present Agentic CLEAR, an automatic, dynamic, and easy-to-use evaluation framework. It produces textual insights into the agent behavior on three levels of granularity: system, trace, and node. Agentic CLEAR operates above the observability layer, enabling seamless integration and featuring an intuitive UI that makes agent evaluation highly accessible. In our experiments on four benchmarks, seven agentic settings, and tens of thousands of LLM calls, we show that Agentic CLEAR produces high-quality, data-driven, insightful feedback. Our analysis shows strong alignment with human-annotated errors and the ability to predict task success rate.

</details>


### [46] [Chinese sensorimotor and embodiment norms for 3,000 lexicalized concepts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22616)
*Jing Chen,Gábor Parti,Yin Zhong,Chu-Ren Huang,Marco Marelli*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文构建了包含3000个汉语词汇的11维感觉运动规范数据库，验证了感知具身强度（PSE）等指标在词汇决策任务中的预测效度，并发现感觉运动信息可从语言分布表征中部分恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概念知识如何植根于身体体验，以及机器系统能否在没有直接感觉运动经验的情况下获取此类知识，是认知科学与具身人工智能的核心问题。大规模规范资源对实证研究至关重要，但非印欧语言中此类资源极为匮乏。

Method: 收集378名汉语母语者对3000个词汇的11维感觉运动评分和单维具身评分；通过信度检验、交叉效度验证、词汇决策任务中的回归分析以及基于语言表征的感觉运动信息可恢复性分析（简单回归模型与表征相似性分析）进行评估。

Result: 评分具有高信度和强交叉效度；PSE-感觉运动指标和Minkowski-3是词汇决策任务的最强预测因子；感觉运动评分可从语言表征中部分恢复（平均斯皮尔曼r=0.62），视觉和听觉维度恢复效果优于化学感知维度；感觉运动空间的几何结构也可部分恢复（r=0.540）。

Conclusion: 分布语言使用编码了具身概念结构的某些方面，感觉运动信息对词汇加工具有促进效应，所构建的数据库为汉语概念具身研究提供了重要资源。

Abstract: Understanding how conceptual knowledge is grounded in bodily experience, and to what extent machine systems can acquire such knowledge without direct sensorimotor experience, are central questions in both cognitive science and embodied artificial intelligence research. Large-scale normative resources are essential for investigating these questions empirically, yet such resources remain sparse for non-Indo-European languages. We present a novel normative database for 3,000 lexicalized concepts in Mandarin Chinese, comprising 11-dimensional sensorimotor ratings and unidimensional embodiment ratings collected from 378 native Mandarin speakers. The ratings demonstrate high reliability and strong cross-norm validity with existing Chinese resources, each of which covers fewer words and a subset of the 11 sensorimotor dimensions. In a validation study, we tested new variables derived from a theoretically motivated metric, Perceptual Strength of Embodiment (PSE) (Huang et al., 2025), together with seven common composite variables, on lexical decision tasks. The results suggest that PSE-Sensorimotor and Minkowski-3 are the strongest composite predictors of lexical decision performance, capturing the facilitatory effects of sensorimotor information on lexical processing. A further exploratory study showed that sensorimotor ratings are substantially recoverable from purely linguistic representations using simple regression models (mean Spearman r = .62 across dimensions), though recovery varied markedly: visual and auditory dimensions yielded higher correspondence than chemosensory ones. Representational similarity analysis further showed that the relational geometry of the sensorimotor space is also partially recoverable (r = .540), consistent with the view that distributional language use encodes aspects of embodied conceptual structure.

</details>


### [47] [More Context, Larger Models, or Moral Knowledge? A Systematic Study of Schwartz Value Detection in Political Texts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22641)
*Víctor Yeste,Paolo Rosso*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了上下文、显性道德知识和模型规模对政治文本中施瓦茨价值观检测的影响，发现更多上下文并非总是更好，检索增强的道德知识一致有效，且模型规模扩大不保证性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 政治文本中的价值观检测因隐式线索依赖周围论证和相邻价值观之间的细微区分而具有挑战性，现有研究对上下文和外部知识的作用缺乏系统评估。

Method: 使用ValuesML/Touché ValueEval格式，比较句子级、窗口级和全文级输入；对比无检索增强与基于 curated 道德知识库的检索增强设置；采用有监督DeBERTa-v3-base/large编码器以及12B至123B参数的零样本大语言模型；测试早期融合、晚期融合和交叉注意力等RAG变体。

Result: 全文上下文使有监督DeBERTa编码器提升3.8-4.8 macro-F1，但对零样本LLM无一致帮助；检索增强的道德知识在匹配比较中一致提升各模型族和上下文条件；从DeBERTa-base到large以及从12B到更大LLM的规模扩展不保证增益；早期融合优于晚期融合和交叉注意力RAG变体；上下文和检索对社会情境性或概念混淆性价值观帮助最大。

Conclusion: 价值敏感的自然语言处理应联合评估上下文、知识和模型族，而非将更长输入或更大模型视为通用改进方案。

Abstract: Detecting Schwartz values in political text is difficult because implicit cues often depend on surrounding arguments and fine-grained distinctions between neighboring values. We study when context and explicit moral knowledge help sentence-level value detection. Using the ValuesML/Touch{é} ValueEval format, we compare sentence, window, and full-document inputs; no-RAG and retrieval-augmented settings with a curated moral knowledge base; supervised DeBERTa-v3-base/large encoders; and zero-shot LLMs from 12B to 123B parameters. The results show that more context is not uniformly better: full-document context improves supervised DeBERTa encoders by 3.8--4.8 macro-F1 points over sentence-only input, but does not consistently help zero-shot LLMs. Retrieved moral knowledge is more consistently useful in matched comparisons, improving each tested model family and context condition under early fusion. However, scaling from DeBERTa-v3-base to large and from 12B to larger LLMs does not guarantee gains, and simple early fusion outperforms the tested late-fusion and cross-attention RAG variants for encoders. Per-value analyses show that context and retrieval help most for socially situated or conceptually confusable values. These findings suggest that value-sensitive NLP should evaluate context, knowledge, and model family jointly rather than treating longer inputs or larger models as universal improvements.

</details>


### [48] [Seeing the Poem: Image-Semantic Detection of AI-Generated Modern Chinese Poetry with MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22654)
*Shanshan Wang,Fengying Ye,Hanjia Lyu,Caiwen Gou,Junchao Wu,Jingming Yao,Chengzhong Xu,Jiebo Luo,Derek F. Wong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种图像-语义引导的现代诗歌检测方法，通过引入与诗歌内容匹配的图像来辅助大语言模型进行检测，实验表明该方法使LLM检测器性能超越传统检测器（包括RoBERTa），Gemini检测器达到85.65%的Macro-F1分数，达到SOTA水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检测研究表明LLM不能有效用作检测器，但这些研究未涉及现代汉语诗歌领域，且尚无相关研究探索LLM在检测现代汉语诗歌方面的性能。

Method: 提出图像-语义引导的诗歌检测方法，通过示例驱动的方式，创新性地引入反映诗歌内容的图像，将图像中的含义、意象和感觉等信息与诗歌文本形成互补判断。

Result: 基于该方法的LLM检测器在性能上优于基于纯文本的基线检测器，甚至超越了最佳传统检测器RoBERTa。Gemini检测器达到85.65%的Macro-F1分数，达到SOTA水平。

Conclusion: 该方法在不同LLM检测器及多种LLM生成数据上均有效，证明了图像-语义引导方法的有效性和通用性。

Abstract: Previous detection studies have shown that LLMs cannot be effectively used as detectors, but these studies have not addressed modern Chinese poetry. Moreover, no relevant research has explored the performance of LLMs in detecting modern Chinese poetry. This paper evaluates and enhances the performance of LLMs as detectors for modern Chinese poetry, and proposes an image-semantic guided poetry detection method. Compared with traditional detection approaches, our method innovatively incorporates images that reflect the content of the poetry. Through example-driven approaches, our method effectively integrates information such as meaning, imagery, and feeling from the image, then forms a complementary judgment with the poem text. Experimental results demonstrate that the LLM detectors based on our method outperform baseline detectors based on plain text, and even surpass the best-performing traditional detector, RoBERTa. The Gemini detector using our method achieves a Macro-F1 score of 85.65%, reaching the state-of-the-art level. The performance improvements of different LLM detectors on multiple LLMs-generated data prove the effectiveness of our method.

</details>


### [49] [Moral Semantics Survive Machine Translation: Cross-Lingual Evidence from Moral Foundations Corpora](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22660)
*Maciej Skorski*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文探究了基于LLM的机器翻译能否弥补道德价值观分类中英语与其他语言（以波兰语为例）之间的标注语料差距。通过约5万条道德标注社交媒体帖子，采用四项验证流程（LaBSE跨语言嵌入相似度、CKA、LLM评判评估、深度学习分类器一致性检验），结果显示尽管在处理俚语、粗俗语和文化负载表达方面存在不足，直接翻译仍能较好保留道德线索（平均余弦相似度0.86，AUC差距0.01-0.02），微调后可进一步缩小差距。证明机器翻译是道德价值观研究在资源匮乏语言中的实用且经济有效的路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 道德语言具有细微差异且随文化变化，而自动道德价值观分类依赖几乎只存在于英语的语言特定标注语料库，其他语言缺乏此类资源。本研究旨在探究能否利用LLM翻译弥合这一资源差距。

Method: 以波兰语为测试用例，使用约5万条来自多种话题的道德标注社交媒体帖子，应用四项验证流程：LaBSE跨语言嵌入相似度、Centered Kernel Alignment (CKA)、LLM-as-judge评估以及深度学习分类器一致性检验。

Result: 直接翻译在处理俚语、粗俗语和文化负载表达方面存在不足，但仍能保留足够细微的道德线索以支持跨语言机器学习。平均余弦相似度达0.86，所有道德基础维度上AUC差距仅为0.01-0.02，且通过语言模型微调可进一步缩小差距。

Conclusion: 机器翻译是面向当前道德价值观研究中资源匮乏语言的一种实用且经济有效的路径，以波兰语为代表的斯拉夫语系验证了该方法，并预期可推广至相关语言。

Abstract: Moral language is subtle and culturally variable, making it difficult to translate faithfully across languages. Idiomatic expressions, slang, and cultural references introduce hard-to-avoid translation artifacts. Yet automated moral values classification depends on language-specific annotated corpora that exist almost exclusively in English. We investigate whether LLM-based translation can bridge this gap, taking Polish as a test case. Using $\sim$50k morally-annotated social media posts from a diverse range of topics, we apply a principled four-method validation pipeline: LaBSE cross-lingual embedding similarity, Centered Kernel Alignment (CKA), LLM-as-judge evaluation, and deep learning classifier parity tests. We show that despite shortcomings in handling slang, vulgarity, and culturally-loaded expressions, direct translation preserves subtle moral cues well enough to be harvested by cross-lingual machine learning -- with mean cosine similarity of 0.86 and AUC gaps of 0.01--0.02 across all foundations closing further under fine-tuning of language models. These results demonstrate that machine translation is a practical and cost-effective path to moral values research in languages currently under-resourced in this domain. We demonstrate this for Polish as a representative Slavic language, with expected generalisation to related languages.

</details>


### [50] [Self-Policy Distillation via Capability-Selective Subspace Projection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22675)
*Guangya Hao,Yitong Shang,Yunbo Long,Zhuokai Zhao,Hanxue Liang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Self-Policy Distillation (SPD)，一种无需外部信号、可泛化且具备能力选择性的大语言模型自蒸馏方法。SPD通过从模型自身在正确性定义令牌上的梯度中提取低秩能力子空间，在自生成过程中将KV激活投影到该子空间，并使用标准下一令牌预测损失对原始输出进行微调。在代码生成、数学推理和多选QA任务上，SPD相比最先进的无外部信号自蒸馏方法提升高达13%，相比预训练基线提升16%，尤其在域外泛化场景下提升15%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自蒸馏方法要么依赖外部信号（如正确性过滤、执行反馈、奖励搜索）来筛选自生成输出——这对最先进的边界模型而言成本高昂且不可用，要么完全跳过筛选直接训练所有原始输出——这种方法往往领域特定且难以泛化。两者还共享一个更深层次的缺陷：自生成输出将任务相关能力与其他因素（如风格模式、格式伪影、模型特定错误）纠缠在一起，削弱了针对特定能力的信号。

Method: SPD包含三个步骤：1) 从模型自身在正确性定义令牌上的梯度中提取低秩能力子空间；2) 在自生成过程中将KV激活投影到该子空间，从而解耦任务相关能力；3) 对生成的原始输出使用标准下一令牌预测损失进行微调。

Result: 在代码生成、数学推理和多选QA任务上，SPD相比最先进的无外部信号自蒸馏方法提升高达13%，相比预训练基线提升16%，在域外泛化设置下提升15%。

Conclusion: SPD是一种无需外部信号、可泛化且具备能力选择性的自蒸馏方法，有效解决了现有方法中信号纠缠和泛化性差的根本问题。

Abstract: Self-distillation bootstraps large language models (LLMs) by training on their own generations. However, existing methods either rely on external signals to curate self-generated outputs (e.g., correctness filtering, execution feedback, and reward search), which are costly and unavailable for the best-performing frontier models, or skip curation entirely and train on all raw outputs, an approach that is often domain-specific and hard to generalize. Both also share a deeper weakness that self-generated outputs entangle task-relevant capability with others, such as stylistic patterns, formatting artifacts, and model-specific errors, diluting the signal for the specific capability one aims to improve. In this paper, we propose Self-Policy Distillation (SPD), which achieves generalizable, capability selective without any external signal. Specifically, SPD extracts a low-rank capability subspace from the model's own gradients on correctness-defining tokens, projects key-value (KV) activations into this subspace during self-generation, and fine-tunes on the resulting raw outputs with standard next-token prediction loss. Through extensive experiments across code generation, mathematical reasoning, and multiple-choice QA, we show that SPD achieves up to 13% improvement over state-of-the-art self-distillation methods without external signals and up to 16% improvement over pre-trained baselines. Notably, SPD demonstrates superior generalizability, achieving 15% better performance under out-of-domain generalization settings.

</details>


### [51] [Tokenization with Split Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22705)
*Craig W. Schmidt,Michael Krumdick,Adam Wiemerslage,Seth Ebner,Varshini Reddy,Yuval Pinter,Chris Tanner*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ToaST是一种基于分裂树的子词分词方法，通过递归推理和整数规划优化词汇表，在英语文本上相比BPE、WordPiece和UnigramLM减少了11%以上的token数量，并在1.5B参数语言模型训练中取得了最优CORE得分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有子词分词方法（如BPE、WordPiece、UnigramLM）在压缩效率和推理token数量方面仍有改进空间，尤其是在大词汇量场景下，需要一种能直接优化压缩率的分词方法以延长模型有效上下文长度。

Method: 提出Tokenization with Split Trees (ToaST)方法：1) 利用预计算的字节n-gram统计，贪心地将每个预分词构建为完整二叉树；2) 推理时递归遍历分裂树，发出每条路径上首个在词汇表中的节点；3) 将词汇表选择形式化为整数规划(IP)，最小化所有分裂树上的总token数；4) LP松弛在实践中近乎整数，可证明得到近似最优词汇表，训练复杂度与分裂树数量呈二次关系。

Result: 在词汇量40960及以上时，ToaST相比BPE、WordPiece和UnigramLM减少超过11%的token数量；降低了常用单字节token的使用频率，显著提升了Renyi效率；在1.5B参数语言模型训练中，CORE得分超越基线2.6%-7.6%，在22个任务中13个取得最佳。

Conclusion: ToaST通过直接优化压缩率的分词策略，在token效率、有效上下文长度和下游任务表现上均优于现有主流子词分词方法，验证了基于分裂树的递归推理与整数规划词汇表选择的有效性。

Abstract: We introduce Tokenization with Split Trees (ToaST), a subword tokenization method that directly optimizes compression under a new recursive inference procedure. ToaST greedily splits each pretoken into a full binary tree using precomputed byte n-gram counts, independent of any vocabulary. Given a vocabulary, inference recursively descends each split tree and emits the first in-vocabulary node reached on each path. Vocabulary selection is formulated as an Integer Program (IP) that minimizes the total token count over all split trees under this inference procedure. The Linear Programming (LP) relaxation is near-integral in practice, yielding provably near-optimal vocabularies, with training time empirically scaling quadratically in the number of split trees. On English text, ToaST reduces token counts by more than 11% compared to BPE, WordPiece, and UnigramLM at vocabulary sizes of 40,960 and above, reducing the number of inference tokens for models using this tokenizer, thus extending the effective context length. ToaST also uses common single-byte tokens less frequently than these baselines, leading to a substantial improvement in Renyi efficiency. In experiments training 1.5B parameter language models, ToaST achieves the highest CORE score, outperforming baselines by 2.6%--7.6%, with significance for two of three, and scoring best on 13 of 22 individual tasks.

</details>


### [52] [ChronoMedKG: A Temporally-Grounded Biomedical Knowledge Graph and Benchmark for Clinical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22734)
*Md Shamim Ahmed,Farzaneh Firoozbakht,Lukas Galke Poech,Jan Baumbach,Richard Röttger*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ChronoMedKG，一个包含460,497个时间关联三元组的时序生物医学知识图谱，覆盖13,431种疾病。通过多智能体流水线从文献中提取证据链接的时序信息（如发病窗口、进展阶段），经多模型共识和可信度过滤构建。在ChronoTQA基准上，前沿LLM从静态问题到时序问题性能下降约30分，而ChronoMedKG检索可挽救47-65%的长尾失败，远超HPOA-RAG的17-29%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生物医学知识图谱（PrimeKG、Hetionet、iKraph等）将疾病关联视为静态事实，缺乏时间编码（如症状在3岁和13岁可能指向不同疾病），限制了其在纵向临床推理和检索增强中的可用性。

Method: 采用疾病自主多智能体流水线，多个前沿LLM独立从PubMed和PMC文献中提取知识，仅保留多模型共识支持、通过可信度过滤和本体对齐的关系。每个关联绑定时间组件（发病窗口、进展阶段）并附有PMID可追溯证据和多信号可信度评分。

Result: ChronoMedKG与Orphadata一致性达92.7%，为6,250种疾病（含1,657种罕见病）添加了HPOA、Orphadata、Phenopackets缺失的时间信息。在ChronoTQA基准中，LLM静态到时序问题性能下降约30分，ChronoMedKG检索挽救47-65%长尾失败，而HPOA-RAG仅17-29%。

Conclusion: ChronoMedKG为检索增强的临床系统提供了此前缺失的关键时间轴，显著优于传统静态KG在时序推理任务上的表现。

Abstract: Biomedical knowledge graphs (KGs) treat disease associations as static facts, but temporal information is crucial for clinical reasoning, e.g., a symptom diagnostic of one disease at age 3 may imply a different disease at age 13. Existing KGs such as PrimeKG, Hetionet, and iKraph do not encode when a finding becomes clinically relevant over the course of a disease. This limits their usefulness for longitudinal clinical reasoning and retrieval augmentation.
  We introduce ChronoMedKG, a temporal biomedical knowledge graph that contains 460,497 evidence-linked triples (filtered from 13M raw extractions) covering 13,431 diseases. Each association is tied to temporal components like onset window or progression stage, which are backed by PMID-traceable evidence and a multi-signal credibility score. The graph is constructed through a disease-autonomous multi-agent pipeline in which multiple frontier LLMs independently extract knowledge from PubMed and PMC literature. Only those relations are kept that are supported by multi-model consensus, survive credibility filtering, as well as ontology alignment.
  ChronoMedKG scored 92.7% agreement against Orphadata and adds temporal grounding for 6,250 diseases absent from HPOA, Orphadata, and Phenopackets, including 1,657 Orphanet-coded rare diseases. We further introduce ChronoTQA, a benchmark of 3,341 questions across eight task types (six temporal plus two static controls), with a 12-question supplementary probe. Frontier LLMs lose roughly 30 points moving from static to temporal questions; ChronoMedKG retrieval rescues 47-65% of their long-tail failures, against 17-29% for HPOA-RAG. As such, ChronoMedKG provides a crucial temporal axis for retrieval-augmented clinical systems that was previously absent.

</details>


### [53] [Understanding Data Temporality Impact on Large Language Models Pre-training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22769)
*Pilchen Hippolyte,Fabre Romain,Signe Talla Franck,Perez Patrick,Grave Edouard*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究预训练数据顺序对LLM时间敏感事实知识获取的影响，通过在时间有序的Common Crawl数据上训练6B参数模型，发现顺序训练能保持通用语言能力的同时提升事实时效性和时间精确性，而随机训练则偏向旧数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM通常在打乱顺序的语料上训练，导致模型知识冻结在训练时点，其时间 grounding 能力尚不清楚。作者希望探究预训练数据顺序如何影响模型获取时间敏感的事实知识。

Method: 1) 构建包含7,000+时间敏感问题的基准测试及评估协议；2) 在时间有序的Common Crawl快照上预训练6B参数模型，与标准随机打乱的预训练进行对比。

Result: 顺序训练的模型在通用语言理解和常识知识上与随机基线持平，但展现出更及时、更时间精确的知识；顺序训练提升了事实新鲜度，而随机训练在旧数据上表现更优（可能由于事实重复更多）。

Conclusion: 时间有序的预训练能有效提升LLM的时间敏感性而不损失通用能力，为LLM的持续学习研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are typically trained on shuffled corpora, yielding models whose knowledge is frozen at train time and whose temporal grounding remains poorly understood. In this work, we study the impact of pre-training dynamics on the acquisition of time-sensitive factual knowledge, focusing specifically on data ordering. Our main contributions are twofold. First, we introduce a comprehensive benchmark of over 7,000 temporally grounded questions and an evaluation protocol that enables analysis of whether models correctly associate facts with their corresponding time periods. Second, we pretrain 6B-parameter models on temporally ordered Common Crawl snapshots and compare them against standard shuffled pre-training. Our results show that sequentially trained models match shuffled baselines on general language understanding and common knowledge while consistently exhibiting more up-to-date and temporally precise knowledge. Temporally ordered pre-training yields improved factual freshness, while shuffled pre-training peaks on older data, possibly due to increased factual repetition. These findings, along with the release of our code at https://github.com/kyutai-labs/kairos , checkpoints, and datasets at https://huggingface.co/collections/kyutai/kairos provide a foundation for future research on continual learning for LLMs.

</details>


### [54] [Reducing Political Manipulation with Consistency Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22771)
*Long Phan,Devin Kim,Alexander Pan,Alice Blair,Adam Khoja,Dan Hendrycks*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现LLMs在处理对立政治话题时存在系统性隐蔽偏见（Covert Political Bias），即对同一议题的不同政治立场采用不对称处理方式。作者识别出7类隐蔽偏见技术，提出情感一致性（Sentiment Consistency）和帮助一致性（Helpfulness Consistency）两项量化指标，并开发了政治一致性训练（PCT）——一种基于强化学习的训练方法，包含情感一致性和帮助一致性训练两个互补范式。实验表明PCT能保持整体帮助性，显著降低隐蔽政治偏见，并泛化到未见过的基准测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多关注LLM的显性政治偏见，但忽略了模型在对待对立政治议题时表现出的不对称性（如修辞、框架、参与深度等方面的系统性差异）。作者旨在揭示并量化这种隐蔽偏见，并提出有效的缓解方法。

Method: ①定义并识别7类隐蔽政治偏见技术；②提出两个量化指标：Sentiment Consistency（情感一致性，衡量修辞与框架对称性）和Helpfulness Consistency（帮助一致性，衡量回答深度与投入的对称性）；③提出Political Consistency Training (PCT)，包含两个RL训练范式：Sentiment Consistency Training和Helpfulness Consistency Training。

Result: PCT在保持模型整体帮助性的前提下，显著降低了隐蔽政治偏见，并且该方法能泛化到训练时未见过的基准测试上。

Conclusion: 隐蔽政治偏见是LLM中一种重要但被忽视的偏见形式，PCT提供了一种有效的缓解方案，能够在不牺牲模型有用性的前提下提升政治中立性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) exhibit systematic political bias across a variety of sensitive contexts. We find that LLMs handle counterpart topics from opposing political sides asymmetrically. We refer to this phenomenon as covert political bias and identify 7 categories of techniques through which it operates. We propose two metrics for covert bias: Sentiment Consistency measures symmetry in rhetoric and framing across paired political prompts; Helpfulness Consistency measures symmetric depth and engagement. To reduce both types of covert bias, we introduce Political Consistency Training (PCT), an RL training method with two complementary paradigms: Sentiment Consistency Training and Helpfulness Consistency Training. We show that PCT preserves overall helpfulness, substantially reduces covert political bias, and generalizes to held-out benchmarks. We release our work at https://political-manipulation.ai

</details>


### [55] [Evaluating Commercial AI Chatbots as News Intermediaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22785)
*Mirac Suzgun,Emily Shen,Federico Bianchi,Alexander Spangher,Thomas Icard,Daniel E. Ho,Dan Jurafsky,James Zou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了6款AI聊天机器人在14天内对2100个基于BBC新闻的事实性问题的回答准确性，涵盖6种语言服务。最佳系统多项选择准确率超90%，但自由回答下降11-13%，全队列下降16-17%。研究发现：印地语准确率最低（79%），存在英语检索偏见；70%以上错误源于检索失败而非推理失败；面对虚假前提问题，准确率从88-96%骤降至19-70%，且存在检测-准确率悖论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI聊天机器人正快速改变人们接触新闻的方式，但尚无研究系统测量这些系统在处理跨语言、跨区域的新兴事实时的准确性，尤其是它们依赖专有搜索集成和检索-合成管线的背景下。

Method: 在14天内（2026年2月9-22日），基于BBC同日新闻报道的2100个事实性问题，对6款AI聊天机器人（Gemini 3 Flash/Pro、Grok 4、Claude 4.5 Sonnet、GPT-5、GPT-4o mini）在6个区域服务（美加、阿拉伯、非洲、印地语、俄语、土耳其语）上进行评估，采用多项选择和自由回答两种评价方式。

Result: 最佳系统多项选择准确率超90%；自由回答下降11-13%，全队列下降16-17%。印地语准确率最低（79% vs 89-91%），引文体现英语检索偏见。70%以上错误源于检索失败。面对虚假前提问题，准确率从88-96%降至19-70%。存在检测-准确率悖论。

Conclusion: 高准确率可能掩盖系统性区域不平等、对检索基础设施的近乎完全依赖，以及对用户提出的不完美查询的脆弱性。

Abstract: AI chatbots are rapidly shaping how people encounter the news, yet no prior study has systematically measured how accurately these systems, with their proprietary search integrations and retrieval-synthesis pipelines, handle emerging facts across languages and regions. We present a 14-day (February 9-22, 2026) evaluation of six AI chatbots (Gemini 3 Flash and Pro, Grok 4, Claude 4.5 Sonnet, GPT-5 and GPT-4o mini) on 2,100 factual questions derived from same-day BBC News reporting across six regional services (US & Canada, Arabic, Afrique, Hindi, Russian, Turkish). The best systems achieve over 90% multiple-choice accuracy on questions about events reported hours earlier. The same systems, however, lose 11-13% under free-response evaluation, and 16-17% across the cohort. We further characterize three failure patterns. First, every model achieves its lowest accuracy on Hindi (79% vs. 89-91% elsewhere) and citations indicate an Anglophone retrieval bias (e.g., models answering Hindi queries cite English Wikipedia more than any Hindi outlet). Second, retrieval, not reasoning, failures drive over 70% of all errors. When models retrieve a correct source, they often extract the correct answer; the problem is to land on the right source in the first place. Third, models achieving 88-96% accuracy on well-formed questions drop to 19-70% when questions contain subtle false premises, with the most vulnerable model accepting fabricated facts 64% of the time. We also identify a detection-accuracy paradox: the best false-premise detector ranks second in adversarial accuracy (abstention rate), while a weaker detector ranks first, showing that premise detection and answer recovery are partially independent capabilities. Overall, these suggest that high accuracy can mask systematic regional inequity, near-total dependence on retrieval infrastructure, and vulnerability to imperfect queries real users pose.

</details>


### [56] [Tokenisation via Convex Relaxations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22821)
*Jan Tempus,Philip Whittington,Craig W. Schmidt,Dennis Komm,Tiago Pimentel*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为ConvexTok的新分词算法，将分词器构建形式化为线性规划问题并用凸优化工具求解，相比BPE和Unigram等贪心算法，能一致提升内在分词指标和语言模型的bits-per-byte，并允许用户验证分词器与最优解的差距（实验表明在常见词汇量下差距在1%以内）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分词算法（如BPE、Unigram）是贪心算法，仅做局部最优决策，未考虑整个词汇表的全局最优性。

Method: 将分词器构建形式化为线性规划问题，使用凸优化工具求解，提出ConvexTok算法。

Result: ConvexTok一致提升了内在分词指标和语言模型的bits-per-byte（BpB），在下游任务上也有提升但不够一致；在常见词汇量下，分词器与最优解的差距在1%以内。

Conclusion: 凸优化方法可以替代贪心算法构建分词器，在保持高效性的同时提供更优的分词质量和可验证的最优性保证。

Abstract: Tokenisation is an integral part of the current NLP pipeline. Current tokenisation algorithms such as BPE and Unigram are greedy algorithms -- they make locally optimal decisions without considering the resulting vocabulary as a whole. We instead formulate tokeniser construction as a linear program and solve it using convex optimisation tools, yielding a new algorithm we call ConvexTok. We find ConvexTok consistently improves intrinsic tokenisation metrics and the bits-per-byte (BpB) achieved by language models; it also improves downstream task performance, but less consistently. Furthermore, ConvexTok allows the user to certify how far their tokeniser is from optimal, with respect to a certain objective, via a lower bound, and we empirically find it to be within 1\% of optimal at common vocabulary sizes.

</details>

### [57] [Temporal Contrastive Transformer for Financial Crime Detection: Self-Supervised Sequence Embeddings via Predictive Contrastive Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21490)
*Danny Butvinik,Yonit Marcus,Nitzan Tal,Gabrielle Azoulay*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出时间对比变换器（TCT），一种自监督对比学习框架，用于从金融交易序列中提取行为时间动态的嵌入表示，以辅助欺诈检测。实验表明，嵌入本身具有预测能力（AUC 0.8644），但与强领域特征结合时无显著提升（AUC 0.9205 vs 0.9245），表明学习到的表征与现有特征抽象高度重叠。该工作处于发展阶段，虽未达到生产就绪，但展示了减少特征工程依赖的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 减少金融犯罪检测中对人工特征工程的依赖，探索自监督时间表征学习从交易序列中自动提取行为模式的可行性。

Method: 提出TCT框架，采用自监督对比学习目标训练Transformer模型，从金融交易序列中编码行为时间动态的嵌入向量；下游任务使用梯度提升分类器评估嵌入质量。

Result: 单独使用嵌入达到AUC 0.8644；结合领域工程特征后AUC为0.9205，与基线（0.9245）无显著差异，说明嵌入与现有特征信息重叠。

Conclusion: TCT能捕获有意义的时序行为信号，但其表征与强领域特征高度重合，未能提供额外增益。工作处于中间阶段，需进一步优化架构、训练目标和集成策略。

Abstract: We introduce the Temporal Contrastive Transformer (TCT), a representation learning framework designed to capture contextual temporal dynamics in sequences of financial transactions. The model is trained using a self-supervised contrastive objective to produce embeddings that encode behavioral patterns over time, with the goal of supporting downstream fraud detection tasks. We evaluate TCT in a realistic setting by using the learned embeddings as input features to a gradient boosting classifier. Experimental results show that embeddings alone achieve meaningful predictive performance (AUC 0.8644), indicating that the model captures non-trivial temporal structure. However, when combined with domain-engineered features, no measurable improvement is observed over the baseline (AUC 0.9205 vs. 0.9245), suggesting that the learned representations largely overlap with existing feature abstractions. These findings position TCT as a promising representation learning approach that captures relevant behavioral signal, while highlighting the challenges of achieving additive value over strong domain features. The results reflect an intermediate stage in the development of temporal representation learning for financial crime detection and motivate further research on model architecture, training objectives, and integration strategies. At this early stage, achieving performance comparable to a strong feature-engineered baseline is itself a meaningful outcome, indicating that learned representations approximate domain-specific features without manual engineering. While not yet production-ready, these results point to a promising direction for reducing reliance on feature engineering in financial crime detection.

</details>


### [58] [Teaching Language Models to Forecast Research Success Through Comparative Idea Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21491)
*Srujan P Mule,Aniketh Garikaparthi,Manasi Patwardhan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了如何使用小型语言模型（SLM）在无需实验的情况下预测研究想法的实证成功。通过构建包含11,488对研究想法的数据集，发现监督微调（SFT）和基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）可显著提升模型预测能力，使8B参数模型准确率达77.1%，超越GPT-5（61.1%），证明了计算高效的小模型可作为有效的客观验证器，为自主科学发现提供可扩展路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着语言模型加速科研自动化，评估和筛选大量AI生成想法成为新瓶颈。当前缺乏在无需详尽实验的情况下预测研究想法成功与否的高效方法。

Method: 构建基于PapersWithCode客观结果的11,488对研究想法数据集；采用监督微调（SFT）和基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）训练语言模型进行比较性经验预测；通过消融实验和分布外测试评估模型鲁棒性。

Result: 现成8B模型准确率仅30%；SFT后提升至77.1%，超越GPT-5的61.1%；RLVR方法达71.35%准确率并提供可解释理由；模型在分布外测试中表现出鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 计算高效的小型语言模型可作为有效的客观验证器，为自主科学发现中自动化想法筛选提供可扩展路径。

Abstract: As language models accelerate scientific research by automating hypothesis generation and implementation, a new bottleneck emerges: evaluating and filtering hundreds of AI-generated ideas without exhaustive experimentation. We ask whether LMs can learn to forecast the empirical success of research ideas before any experiments are run. We study comparative empirical forecasting: given a benchmark-specific research goal and two candidate ideas, predict which will achieve better benchmark performance. We construct a dataset of 11,488 idea pairs grounded in objective outcomes from PapersWithCode. While off-the-shelf 8B-parameter models struggle (30% acc.), SFT dramatically boosts performance to 77.1%, outperforming GPT-5 (61.1%). By framing evaluation as a reasoning task via Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR), we train models to discover latent reasoning paths, achieving 71.35% acc. with interpretable justifications. Through additional ablations and out-of-distribution tests, we show robustness to surface-level heuristics and transfer to both a cross-domain time-split test set and an independently constructed test set. Our results demonstrate that compute-efficient small language models can serve as effective, objective verifiers, offering a scalable path for autonomous scientific discovery.

</details>


### [59] [Double descent for least-squares interpolation on contaminated data: A simulation study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21494)
*Tino Werner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了过参数化线性回归模型中，面对污染训练数据时是否会出现"双下降"现象。结果表明，高度非鲁棒的最小二乘插值估计器在过参数化程度足够高时确实表现出双下降，其泛化性能甚至超越鲁棒替代方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在训练数据被污染（存在异常值）的线性回归场景中，过参数化模型是否依然能展现出双下降现象，从而理解鲁棒统计与过参数化泛化之间的相互作用。

Method: 对比高度非鲁棒的最小二乘插值估计器与多种鲁棒替代方法在污染训练数据上的泛化性能，分析随模型复杂度变化时的误差曲线。

Result: 过参数化程度足够大时，最小二乘插值估计器确实表现出双下降现象，且其泛化性能优于鲁棒替代方法。

Conclusion: 尽管最小二乘估计器在经典鲁棒统计中表现脆弱，但在过参数化场景下，其泛化性能反而超越专门设计的鲁棒方法，这为理解过参数化模型的鲁棒性提供了新视角。

Abstract: Overparametrized models can exhibit an excellent generalization performance, although they should be prone to overfitting according to classical statistical theory. The discovery of the "double descent", indicating that the generalization error decreases after a certain model complexity has been reached, opened a new line of research. Robust statistics considers statistical estimation on contaminated data, which, due to assumptions that do not hold on real data, let data points appear as outliers w.r.t. the assumed "ideal" distribution, potentially severely distorting any classical estimator. We address the question whether a double descent phenomenon can be observed in a linear regression setting with contaminated training data. We compare the performance of the highly non-robust least-squares interpolation estimator with several robust alternatives. It turns out that large overparametrization indeed allows for a double descent phenomenon, resulting in a very good generalization performance of the least-squares interpolator, surpassing that of the robust alternatives.

</details>


### [60] [HealthCraft: A Reinforcement Learning Safety Environment for Emergency Medicine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21496)
*Brandon Dent*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HealthCraft 是首个基于真实急诊医学条件的公开强化学习环境，通过 FHIR R4 世界状态、24 个 MCP 工具和双层奖励机制评估前沿语言模型的轨迹级安全性。在 205 个任务上，Claude Opus 4.6 和 GPT-5.4 的 Pass@1 分别仅为 24.8% 和 12.6%，安全失败率高达 27.5% 和 34.0%，多步骤工作流性能近乎崩溃（1.0% 和 0.0%）。环境、任务和评估工具已开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有静态医疗 QA 基准无法捕捉急诊医学中关键的失败模式（轨迹级安全崩溃、工具误用、持续临床压力下的妥协），而前沿语言模型正在以超出安全评估基础设施建设的速度部署到临床流程中，亟需能够评估轨迹级安全性的强化学习环境。

Method: 基于 Corecraft 改编，构建 FHIR R4 世界状态（14 种实体类型、3,987 个种子实体），暴露 24 个 MCP 工具，设计双层奖励机制（违反安全关键标准即归零奖励）。发布 195 个任务（后扩展至 205 个），共 2,337 个二值评估标准（其中 515 个安全关键），并使用确定性 LLM 裁判覆盖层控制评估噪声。

Result: Claude Opus 4.6 Pass@1 24.8% [21.5-28.4]，安全失败率 27.5%；GPT-5.4 Pass@1 12.6% [10.2-15.6]，安全失败率 34.0%。多步骤工作流性能崩塌（Claude 1.0%，GPT-5.4 0.0%）。v2 到 v8 修复的 6 个基础设施漏洞足以改变模型排名顺序。

Conclusion: 现有前沿模型在急诊医学场景下性能严重不足，安全失败率高，多步骤轨迹级任务几乎不可用。基础设施保真度严重影响评估结果。奖励信号尚不能直接用于训练（约束条件通过率 0.929 存在可博弈性），训练奖励消融实验留待未来工作。

Abstract: Frontier language models are being deployed into clinical workflows faster than the infrastructure to evaluate them safely. Static medical-QA benchmarks miss the failure modes that matter in emergency medicine: trajectory-level safety collapse, tool misuse, and capitulation under sustained clinical pressure. We present HealthCraft, the first public reinforcement-learning environment that rewards trajectory-level safety under realistic emergency-medicine conditions, adapted from Corecraft. It is built on a FHIR R4 world state with 14 entity types and 3,987 seed entities, exposes 24 MCP tools, and defines a dual-layer rubric that zeroes reward whenever any safety-critical criterion is violated. We release 195 tasks across six categories, graded against 2,255 binary criteria (515 safety-critical); a post-hoc 10-task negative-class slate extends this to 205 tasks and 2,337 criteria. V8 results on two frontier models show Claude Opus 4.6 at Pass@1 24.8% [21.5-28.4] and GPT-5.4 at 12.6% [10.2-15.6], with safety-failure rates of 27.5% and 34.0%. On multi-step workflows - the closest proxy to real emergency care - performance collapses to near zero (Claude 1.0%, GPT-5.4 0.0%) despite partial competence on individual steps. Six infrastructure bugs fixed between pilots v2 and v8 re-ordered which model "looks stronger," evidence that infrastructure fidelity is part of the measurement. A deterministic LLM-judge overlay bounds evaluator noise, and a 60-run negative-class smoke pilot shows the reward signal is not drop-in training-safe: restraint criteria pass at 0.929 prevalence, a gameability an eval harness can tolerate but a training reward cannot. We scaffold coupling to a Megatron+SGLang+GRPO loop per Corecraft Section 5.2 and leave training-reward ablations as future work. Environment, tasks, rubrics, and harness are released under Apache 2.0.

</details>


### [61] [Harnesses for Inference-Time Alignment over Execution Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21516)
*Boyuan Wang,Bochao Li,Minghan Wang,Yuxin Tao,Fang Kong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从推理时轨迹对齐视角研究LLM Agent的哈内斯（harness）设计，将哈内斯分解为任务分解与引导执行两种机制，量化了工作流粒度、重试预算和引导动作重加权对性能的影响，揭示了过度分解、过度剪枝和幻觉执行等失败模式，并发现部分哈内斯（仅指定初始步骤）可优于完全结构化的工作流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 哈内斯工程虽被广泛用于提升LLM Agent的长期性能，但更复杂的哈内斯并不一定更好——增加分解或引导有时改善执行，有时反而降低任务成功率。现有研究缺乏对哈内斯设计中性能边界的系统理解。

Method: 提出推理时轨迹对齐视角，将哈内斯分离为任务分解（将任务结构化为子目标）和引导执行（在执行过程中重塑局部动作分布）两种机制；通过理论量化工作流粒度、重试预算和引导动作重加权对性能的影响；通过合成实验和真实终端Agent基准验证。

Result: 识别出过度分解、过度剪枝和幻觉执行等具体失败模式；发现仅指定初始步骤的部分哈内斯在通过率上可超越完全结构化的工作流。

Conclusion: 哈内斯设计的有效形式可以是部分的、不完整的——仅引导初始步骤并将剩余执行交给Agent自主完成，反而可能取得更好效果。

Abstract: Harness engineering has emerged as an important inference-time technique for large language model (LLM) agents, aiming to improve long-term performance through task decomposition and guided execution. However, more elaborate harnesses are not uniformly better: increasing decomposition or guidance can sometimes improve execution, but can also reduce final task success. We study harness design through the lens of inference-time trajectory alignment. This perspective separates harness into two mechanisms: task decomposition, which structures a task into sub-goals, and guided execution, which reshapes local action distributions during execution. This decomposition allows us to quantify how workflow granularity, retry budgets, and guidance-induced action reweighting shape the performance limits of harness design. It further reveals concrete failure modes, including over-decomposition, over-pruning, and hallucinated execution. We validate these predictions through controlled synthetic experiments and real terminal agent benchmarks. Inspired by the theory, we further show that effective harnesses can be partial: specifying only the initial steps and leaving the remaining execution to agent can achieve higher pass rate than fully structured workflows.

</details>


### [62] [A Reproducible Log-Driven AutoML Framework for Interpretable Pipeline Optimization in Healthcare Risk Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21528)
*Rui Huang,Lican Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出yvsoucom-iterkit，一个确定性、日志驱动的自动化机器学习框架，将pipeline优化编码为可追溯的配置级系统。在Pima印第安人糖尿病和Stroke数据集上评估超过18,000种pipeline配置，发现搜索空间结构化的部分冗余特性，性能主要由少数交互组件决定。集成模型表现稳定（Weighted-F1达0.89-0.94），跨种子分析揭示了性能-鲁棒性权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确可复现的疾病风险预测面临异质性特征、有限样本和严重类别不平衡等挑战，现有AutoML方法缺乏确定性和可追溯性。

Method: 提出yvsoucom-iterkit框架，将pipeline优化编码为可追溯的日志实体；通过随机森林重要性分析、组件相似性分析和跨种子鲁棒性分析进行系统评估。

Result: Pima数据集上，augmentation(0.454)、model choice(0.198)和imbalance handling(0.101)是关键驱动因素；Stroke数据集上imbalance handling占主导(0.406)。组件间存在强冗余（biMax-biMean RMS距离0.0252，mixup与无增广距离0.0279）。集成模型Weighted-F1达0.89(Pima)和0.94(Stroke)，跨种子变异系数0.023-0.026。

Conclusion: 有效的AutoML优化可以聚焦于少量高影响力的组件；集成模型在性能和鲁棒性之间取得较好平衡。

Abstract: Accurate and reproducible disease risk prediction remains challenging due to heterogeneous features, limited samples, and severe class imbalance. This study introduces yvsoucom-iterkit, a deterministic and log-driven automated machine learning framework that formulates pipeline optimization as a fully reproducible, configuration-level system. Each pipeline is encoded as a traceable log entity, enabling analysis of component attribution, interactions, similarity, and cross-seed robustness. Experiments on the Pima Indians Diabetes and Stroke datasets across more than 18,000 pipeline configurations reveal a structured and partially redundant search space, where performance is governed by a small subset of interacting components. Random Forest importance analysis identifies augmentation (0.454), model choice (0.198), and imbalance handling (0.101) as key drivers on Pima, while imbalance handling dominates Stroke (0.406). Component similarity analysis shows strong redundancy, with feature selection variants (biMax-biMean) exhibiting low RMS distance (0.0252), mixup closely matching no augmentation (0.0279), and TomekLinks aligning with no imbalance handling (0.0325), whereas Gaussian noise shows greater divergence from no augmentation (0.10). The framework achieves strong and stable performance using ensemble models (Weighted-F1 0.89, Macro-F1 0.88 on Pima; Weighted-F1 0.94 on Stroke), while Macro-F1 remains lower on Stroke (0.67) due to class imbalance. Cross-seed analysis reveals a performance-robustness trade-off, with ensembles showing lower variability (0.023-0.026) than SVM. These results indicate that effective AutoML optimization can focus on a reduced set of high-impact components.

</details>


### [63] [DualOptim+: Bridging Shared and Decoupled Optimizer States for Better Machine Unlearning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21539)
*Xuyang Zhong,Qizhang Li,Yiwen Guo,Chen Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DualOptim+框架，通过基态和增量态架构优化大语言模型中的机器遗忘，在遗忘与保留目标间实现更好权衡，并提供了8bit量化版本以减少内存开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决大语言模型机器遗忘中遗忘梯度与保留梯度之间存在方向冲突，导致难以同时兼顾两个目标的优化难题。

Method: 引入基态（base state）捕获遗忘与保留目标的共享表示，以及增量态（delta states）保留各目标特有的残差；优化器根据遗忘与保留梯度间的方向冲突，自适应地桥接共享状态与解耦状态。同时提出DualOptim+ 8bit量化变体降低内存开销。

Result: 在虚构数据遗忘、真实世界遗忘、安全对齐和多任务学习等任务上，DualOptim+始终在不同目标之间实现了更优的权衡。

Conclusion: DualOptim+是一种有效的优化框架，能够在遗忘与保留目标之间取得更好的平衡，量化版本在不牺牲性能的前提下减少内存占用。

Abstract: We propose DualOptim+, a novel optimization framework for improving machine unlearning in large language models. It introduces a base state to capture common representations shared by forgetting and retaining objectives and delta states to preserve objective-specific residuals. This architecture allows the optimizer to adaptively bridge shared and decoupled states based on the directional conflict between forgetting and retaining gradients. We further introduce DualOptim+ 8bit, a quantized variant that reduces memory overhead without compromising performance. Extensive experiments across fictitious and real-world unlearning, safety alignment, and multi-task learning tasks demonstrate that DualOptim+ consistently achieves a superior trade-off between different objectives. Codes are available at https://github.com/CityU-MLO/DualOptimPlus.

</details>


### [64] [Provable Joint Decontamination for Benchmarking Multiple Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21543)
*Zhenlong Liu,Hao Zeng,Hongxin Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出联合包络共形选择方法(JECS)，通过共形p值聚合与自适应BH校正，在多模型基准去污染中实现全局污染率控制，在保持理论保证的同时比基线方法具有更高检测效力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准数据污染检测方法中，基于分数的技术缺乏理论保证；而针对单模型的共形方法虽然能控制错误发现，但分别应用于每个模型会产生模型特定的基准，损害跨模型公平比较。

Method: 1) 计算每个模型的共形p值；2) 按数据点取各模型p值的最大值做聚合；3) 从右尾观测数据中重建最大p零分布的保守包络；4) 对包络重缩放后的值应用自适应Benjamini-Hochberg过程，选择具有全局污染率控制的基准。

Result: 在多种模型和基准上的大量实验表明，JECS在持续维持目标全局污染率控制的同时，实现了比最大p基线更高的检测效力。

Conclusion: JECS形式化了多模型基准去污染为联合选择问题，为跨模型公平比较提供了有理论保证的解决方案。

Abstract: Benchmark data contamination has become a central challenge in LLM evaluation: when evaluation examples appear in the training data of one or more audited models, reported performance can be inflated and cross-model comparisons become unreliable. A broad line of training-data detection work designs scores to quantify how strongly a model memorizes a given data point, but these score-based methods lack theoretical guarantees. Recent conformal approaches provide provable false-identification control for a single model; however, applying them separately to each model can produce model-specific benchmarks, undermining fair comparison across models. In this work, we formalize multi-model benchmark decontamination as a joint selection problem and propose Joint Envelope Conformal Selection (JECS), a conformal procedure that enables global contamination rate (GCR) control under stated assumptions. Specifically, JECS computes per-model conformal p-values, aggregates them by the per-item maximum, and reconstructs a conservative envelope of the max-p null distribution from right-tail observations above a data-driven threshold. By applying the adaptive Benjamini-Hochberg (BH) procedure to the envelope-rescaled values, we select a benchmark with provable GCR control. Extensive experiments across various models and benchmarks demonstrate that JECS achieves higher power than the max-p baseline while consistently maintaining the target GCR control.

</details>


### [65] [Tabular foundation models for robust calibration of near-infrared chemical sensing data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21544)
*Robin Reiter,Denis Cornet,Fabien Michel,Lauriane Rouan,Gregory Beurier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文评估了表格基础模型TabPFN在近红外光谱化学传感中的校准性能，在66个NIR数据集上对比了多种方法，结果显示预处理优化的TabPFN在回归任务中表现最佳，直接使用原始光谱的TabPFN在分类任务中表现最优，但在光谱异常值和外推样本上优势减弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NIR光谱传感器的实际部署依赖于能够处理高维、共线性光谱、有限样本量、预处理依赖、光谱异常值及外推问题的校准模型，现有方法存在局限性，需要探索新的校准策略。

Method: 利用TabPFN作为表格基础模型，在66个NIR数据集上（54个回归+12个分类任务）进行基准测试；对比直接推理原始光谱与预处理优化推理两种方式；与PLS/PLS-DA、Ridge、CatBoost、一维CNN等方法比较；采用统一的验证框架（预处理和模型选择仅在校准数据上完成）。

Result: 回归任务中，预处理优化的TabPFN整体平均排名最优，显著优于PLS、CatBoost、原始光谱TabPFN和CNN-1D，与Ridge统计上相当；分类任务中，直接使用原始光谱的TabPFN平均排名最佳。在光谱异常值和外推样本上优势减小，经典化学计量学模型仍具竞争力。

Conclusion: 表格基础模型可以补充现有的NIR化学传感化学计量学工作流，尤其适用于小到中等规模的校准场景，同时需要针对光谱数据的特定先验知识和不确定性感知部署策略。

Abstract: Near-infrared spectroscopy is increasingly used as a rapid, non-destructive chemical sensing technology for the analysis of food, pharmaceutical, biological, and environmental samples. However, the practical deployment of NIR sensors still depends on calibration models able to handle high-dimensional, collinear spectra, limited sample sizes, preprocessing dependence, spectral outliers, and extrapolation beyond the calibration domain. Here, we evaluate whether tabular foundation models can provide a new calibration strategy for NIR chemical sensing. We benchmark TabPFN on 66 NIR datasets covering 54 regression and 12 classification tasks, and compare direct inference on raw spectra with preprocessing-optimized inference against PLS/PLS-DA, Ridge, Catboost, and one-dimensional convolutional neural networks. The study uses a unified validation framework in which preprocessing and model selection are performed exclusively on calibration data before external test evaluation. In regression, preprocessing-optimized TabPFN achieves the best overall average rank and significantly outperforms PLS, CatBoost, TabPFN on raw spectra, and CNN-1D, while remaining statistically comparable to Ridge. In classification, TabPFN applied directly to raw spectra provides the best average rank, with performance close to the optimized variant. Robustness analyses show that TabPFN provides strong average predictive performance but that its advantage decreases on spectral outliers and extrapolated samples, where classical chemometric models remain competitive. These results suggest that tabular foundation models can complement established chemometric workflows for NIR chemical sensing, especially in small- to medium-sized calibration settings, while highlighting the need for spectroscopy-specific priors and uncertainty-aware deployment strategies.

</details>


### [66] [Expectation Consistency Loss: Rethink Confidence Calibration under Covariate Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21552)
*Jinzong Dong,Zhaohui Jiang,Bo Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对协变量偏移下的置信度校准问题，提出了期望一致性条件（Expectation consistency condition），并据此设计了期望一致性损失（ECL），该损失无需重要性加权，兼容多种校准方式，且在理论和实验上均验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有置信度校准方法大多假设训练与测试数据独立同分布，在协变量偏移下失效；而已有的协变量偏移校准方法存在类别级/规范级校准困难、依赖不稳定重要性加权等局限性。

Method: 首先推导了协变量偏移下置信度校准的充要条件——期望一致性条件，揭示协变量偏移不一定导致校准失效；随后基于该条件提出无监督域适应损失ECL，兼容规范校准、类别级校准和顶标签校准；最后理论上证明了ECL的样本复杂度与ECE一致，并给出了可训练的mini-batch方案。

Result: 在模拟和真实协变量偏移数据集上验证了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 期望一致性条件比全局协变量分布对齐更弱，为协变量偏移下的置信度校准提供了新的理论基础和实用方法。

Abstract: Confidence calibration for classification models is vital in safety-critical decision-making scenarios and has received extensive attention. General confidence calibration methods assume training and test data are independent and identically distributed, limiting their effectiveness under covariate shifts. Previous calibration methods under covariate shift struggle with class-wise or canonical calibrations and often rely on unstable importance weighting when density ratios are large or unbounded. Given the above limitations, this paper rethinks confidence calibration under covariate shifts. First, we derive a necessary and sufficient condition for confidence calibration under covariate shifts, named Expectation consistency condition, which reveals covariate shifts do not necessarily lead to uncalibrated confidence and provides a weaker condition for confidence calibration than global covariate distribution alignment. Then, utilizing Expectation consistency condition, this paper proposes an unsupervised domain adaptation loss to calibrate confidence of the target domain, named Expectation consistency loss (ECL), which is compatible with canonical calibration, class-wise calibration, and top-label calibration. Third, we prove that computing ECL loss has the same sample complexity as Expected Calibration Error (ECE) and provide a theoretically grounded mini-batch trainable scheme for ECL loss. Finally, we validate the effectiveness of our method on both simulated and real-world covariate shift datasets.

</details>


### [67] [Beyond Single Slot: Joint Optimization for Multi-Slot Guaranteed Display Advertising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21556)
*Zhaoqi Zhang,Jiaming Deng,Miao Xie,Linyou Cai,Qianlong Xie,Xingxing Wang,Siqiang Luo,Gao Cong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种多槽位保证展示广告的联合优化框架，通过合同轮盘机制和页面浏览量约束解决槽位冗余、合同失衡和曝光集中问题，在美团广告平台上线后实现ARPU提升28.99%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有保证展示广告方法大多基于单槽位假设，无法有效优化多槽位页面浏览场景下的广告分配，存在槽位冗余、合同失衡和曝光集中等挑战。

Method: 将多槽位GD分配建模为离线二分图匹配问题，引入合同轮盘机制处理槽位独占性，加入页面浏览量约束控制曝光次数，并提出可扩展的分配优化算法支持大规模部署。

Result: 在美团广告平台的线上A/B测试中，70%流量下平均每用户收入提升28.99%，DID分析显示合同稳定性改善。

Conclusion: 该框架显著提升了商家ROI、平台收入效率和合同履约鲁棒性，在实际广告部署中具有强适用性和有效性。

Abstract: Guaranteed display advertising is crucial for platform monetization, yet existing methods often operate under a single-slot assumption, limiting their ability to optimize allocation across multi-slot page views. In this paper, we propose a novel joint optimization framework for multi-slot GD allocation, addressing key challenges such as slot-level redundancy, contract imbalance, and exposure concentration. Our approach formulates the allocation as an offline bipartite matching problem with a contract roulette mechanism for slot exclusivity and Page View constraints for impression control, and incorporates a scalable allocation optimization algorithm for efficient large-scale deployment. Extensive online tests on the Meituan advertising platform demonstrate that our method significantly improves merchant ROI, platform revenue efficiency, and contract fulfillment robustness. Specifically, online A/B tests show a 28.99% increase in Average Revenue Per User under 70% traffic, and DID analysis further indicates improved contract stability, demonstrating the strong applicability and effectiveness of our framework in real-world advertising deployments.

</details>


### [68] [Leveraging Self-Paced Curriculum Learning for Enhanced Modality Balance in Multimodal Conversational Emotion Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21565)
*Phuong-Anh Nguyen,The-Son Le,Duc-Trong Le,Cam-Van Thi Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于自步课程学习（SPCL）的即插即用框架，通过双层次难度评估器（话语级和对话级）和学习调度器，有效解决了多模态情感识别中的模态不对齐和失衡学习问题，在IEMOCAP和MELD数据集上取得了显著且一致的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态情感识别中存在着模态不对齐（modality misalignment）和失衡学习（imbalanced learning）两大挑战，限制了多模态信息的有效利用。

Method: 提出一个即插即用的SPCL框架，包含：(1) 双层次难度评估器（Dual-level Difficulty Measurer），从话语级（细粒度模态特异性难度）和对话级（情感依赖性与模态连贯性）两个层面评估样本难度；(2) 学习调度器（Learning Scheduler），根据难度得分动态引导模型从简单样本到困难样本进行训练。

Result: 在IEMOCAP上，加权F1提升约+1.2%至+6.6%；在MELD上，提升最高达+10.4%。实验表明该方法在不同架构和模态设置下均有一致改进。

Conclusion: SPCL作为一种轻量级即插即用模块，能有效缓解模态失衡问题并提升模型鲁棒性，具有良好的通用性和有效性。

Abstract: Multimodal Emotion Recognition in Conversations (MERC) is a crucial task for understanding human interactions, where multimodal approaches integrating language, facial expressions, and vocal tone have achieved significant progress. However, modality misalignment and imbalanced learning remain major challenges, limiting the effective utilization of multimodal information. To address this issue, we propose a plug-and-play framework based on Self-Paced Curriculum Learning (SPCL) for MERC. We introduce a dual-level Difficulty Measurer that captures both utterance-level and conversation-level challenges. The utterance-level score models fine-grained modality-specific difficulty, while the conversation-level score captures broader dialogue structures, including emotional dependencies and modality coherence. Based on these scores, the Learning Scheduler dynamically guides training from easier to more difficult instances. By integrating SPCL into existing MERC architectures, our method alleviates modality imbalance and improves model robustness. Extensive experiments on the IEMOCAP and MELD datasets demonstrate consistent improvements across different architectures and modality settings. On IEMOCAP, SPCL improves weighted F1-score by approximately +1.2% to +6.6% over baseline models, while on MELD, gains reach up to +10.4%. These results highlight the effectiveness and generalizability of SPCL as a lightweight plug-and-play module for multimodal emotion recognition.

</details>


### [69] [Calibration, Uncertainty Communication, and Deployment Readiness in CKD Risk Prediction: A Framework Evaluation Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21566)
*Michael O. Eniolade*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 五种机器学习模型在UCI CKD数据集上表现完美（AUROC 1.00），但迁移到MIMIC-IV外部数据集后，AUROC降至0.48-0.58，校准误差飙升至0.68-0.76，共形预测覆盖率从0.80-0.98暴跌至0.21-0.25，部署就绪度评分均不超过4/16，表明内部完美性能无法泛化到外部数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CKD风险预测模型过度关注区分度而忽视校准与不确定性量化，导致临床决策中概率输出不可靠，缺乏外部验证下的部署就绪度评估。

Method: 在UCI CKD数据集（400例，62.5%患病率）上训练逻辑回归、随机森林、XGBoost、Platt缩放SVM和高斯朴素贝叶斯五种分类器；采用Platt缩放和等渗回归进行校准；通过分裂共形预测量化不确定性（目标90%边际覆盖率）；在MIMIC-IV队列（97例，23.7%患病率）上进行分布压力测试，评估患病率偏移和特征缺失下的表现；使用八维度部署就绪度框架评估。

Result: 内部测试集AUROC均为1.00，等渗校准后ECE降至0.000-0.022；外部MIMIC-IV上AUROC降至0.48-0.58，ECE升至0.68-0.76，共形覆盖率从0.80-0.98降至0.21-0.25（目标90%）；部署就绪度评分均≤4/16。

Conclusion: 近乎完美的内部性能无法迁移到外部数据，临床预测模型在部署前必须在外部数据上评估校准稳定性和共形预测覆盖率。

Abstract: Machine learning models for chronic kidney disease (CKD) risk prediction often post strong discrimination scores on internal test sets. Calibration and uncertainty quantification get far less attention, leaving clinicians without reliable information about whether the probability outputs are accurate.
  We trained five classifiers on the UCI CKD dataset (400 patients, 62.5% CKD prevalence): logistic regression, random forest, XGBoost, SVM with Platt scaling, and Gaussian naive Bayes. We evaluated each across calibration quality, conformal prediction coverage, and an eight-criterion deployment readiness framework. A distributional stress-test applied the best-calibrated variant of each model to the open-access MIMIC-IV demo cohort (97 patients, 23.7% CKD) to assess behaviour under prevalence shift and feature missingness. We measured calibration before and after Platt scaling and isotonic regression using Expected Calibration Error and Brier Score, and quantified uncertainty through split conformal prediction targeting 90% marginal coverage.
  All five models reached AUROC 1.00 on the UCI test set. Isotonic recalibration reduced internal ECE to 0.000-0.022. On MIMIC-IV, AUROC fell to 0.48-0.58, ECE rose to 0.68-0.76, and conformal coverage dropped from 0.80-0.98 to 0.21-0.25 against a 90% target. No model scored above 4 out of 16 on the deployment readiness checklist.
  Near-perfect internal performance did not transfer. Calibration stability and conformal coverage should be evaluated on external data before any clinical prediction model moves toward deployment.

</details>


### [70] [ConTact: Contact-First Antibody CDR Design via Explicit Interface Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21600)
*Mansoor Ahmed,Spencer VonBank,Nadeem Taj,Sujin Lee,Naila Jan,Murray Patterson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ConTact方法，将抗体CDR设计分解为三个级联阶段（表面互补指纹学习、CDR-抗原接触预测、接触门控抗原特征注入），通过距离偏置交叉注意力和接触加权损失，在CHIMERA-Bench数据集上取得最优结构质量和表位感知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有抗体CDR设计方法将"识别哪些CDR位置会接触抗原"和"在这些位置选择氨基酸"两个子问题混为一谈，导致模型通过统一的信号传递隐式学习接触推理，抗原信号被均匀稀释到所有位置。

Method: 提出ConTact框架，采用"接触-然后-动作"架构：(1) 学习表面互补指纹；(2) 预测CDR-抗原接触；(3) 将接触门控的抗原特征注入序列头部。包含距离偏置交叉注意力模块编码几何先验，以及接触加权交叉熵损失将梯度信号集中在结合关键位置。

Result: 在CHIMERA-Bench数据集上，ConTact在结构质量上提升7% RMSD，表位感知F1分数比GNN基线提升10%，序列恢复AAR达到0.38。

Conclusion: 显式解耦接触识别与氨基酸选择两个子问题能够有效提升抗体CDR设计的性能，验证了"接触-然后-动作"范式的有效性。

Abstract: Computational antibody CDR design methods condition on antigen structure to generate binding loops, yet existing architectures conflate two fundamentally distinct sub-problems: identifying which CDR positions will contact the antigen, and selecting amino acids at those positions. This conflation forces models to learn contact reasoning implicitly through uniform message passing, diluting antigen signal across all positions equally. We introduce ConTact, a contact-then-act architecture that explicitly decomposes CDR design into three cascaded stages: learning surface complementarity fingerprints, predicting CDR-antigen contacts, and injecting contact-gated antigen features into the sequence head. A distance-biased cross-attention module encodes geometric priors favoring spatial neighbors, while a contact-weighted cross-entropy loss concentrates gradient signal on binding-critical positions. On CHIMERA-Bench dataset, ConTact achieves the best structural quality (7% RMSD improvement over the next-best baseline), best epitope awareness (10% F1 score over GNN baselines), and competitive sequence recovery (AAR 0.38) among several CDR-H3 design baselines.

</details>


### [71] [When Are Teacher Tokens Reliable? Position-Weighted On-Policy Self-Distillation for Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21606)
*Xiaogeng Liu,Xinyan Wang,Yingzi Ma,Yechao Zhang,Chaowei Xiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出位置加权的自蒸馏方法（PW-OPSD），通过诊断发现推理过程中教师令牌的可靠性具有轨迹结构特征，并利用位置分数替代传统熵指标进行加权，显著提升数学推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有on-policy自蒸馏对所有令牌均匀加权，而基于熵的方法在推理场景中失效，因为高熵可能表示良性解多样性而非不可靠性。作者希望识别并利用教师令牌可靠性的结构特征来改进蒸馏效果。

Method: 提出分支可行性诊断方法：记录教师回答提示中的下一个令牌替代方案，在学生对策略脊柱前缀后强制每个替代方案，测试学生模板续写是否恢复正确答案。基于此发现位置分数是预测教师令牌可靠性的最强指标（AUROC=0.83），进而提出PW-OPSD，在保持相同学生轨迹、特权教师传递和裁剪前向KL目标的基础上，增加位置权重。

Result: PW-OPSD在AIME 2024和AIME 2025上Avg@12分别提升+1.0和+1.1；在DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Llama-8B和Olmo-3-7B-Think上的泛化评估也显示一致的Avg@12改进。

Conclusion: 教师令牌在推理蒸馏中的可靠性具有轨迹级别的结构特征，可通过位置权重有效利用，无需额外教师计算。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) trains a student on its own rollouts using a privileged teacher, but its standard objective weights all generated tokens equally, implicitly treating the privileged teacher target as equally reliable at every student-visited prefix. Existing entropy-based OPD methods relax this uniformity by modulating token-level supervision with teacher entropy, but high teacher entropy in reasoning has an ambiguous reliability meaning: it can reflect either non-viable uncertainty or benign solution diversity. To identify this phenomenon, we introduce a branch-viability diagnostic. Specifically, we record next-token alternatives from the privileged-answer teacher prompt, force each alternative after the student prompt plus its on-policy spine prefix, and test whether the resulting student-template continuation recovers the correct answer. On Qwen3-4B, we find that an oriented within-sequence position score is the strongest tested predictor of teacher-token reliability, reaching an area-under-ROC-curve (AUROC) of 0.83; local uncertainty scores are at most 0.57. Motivated by this trajectory-level structure, we propose Position-Weighted On-Policy Self-Distillation (PW-OPSD), which applies an increasing position weight while keeping the same student rollout, privileged teacher pass, and clipped forward-KL target as OPSD. In our comprehensive evaluations with different random seeds, the diagnostic-derived PW-OPSD improves AIME 2024 and AIME 2025 Avg@12 by +1.0 and +1.1 points, and a generalization evaluation on two larger-scale models from different families, DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Llama-8B and Olmo-3-7B-Think, also demonstrates consistent aggregate Avg@12 improvements. These results show that teacher-token reliability in reasoning distillation is trajectory-structured and can be utilized without additional teacher computation.

</details>


### [72] [$\textit{BlockFormer}$ : Transformer-based inference from interaction maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21617)
*Eloïse Touron,Pedro L. C. Rodrigues,Julyan Arbel,Nelle Varoquaux,Michael Arbel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Transformer架构的数据驱动方法，利用模拟器生成合成数据训练模型，从Hi-C相互作用图中推断可变数量和尺寸的实体参数，成功应用于多种物种的着丝粒定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将着丝粒识别等相互作用图推理问题形式化为通用逆问题，应对实体数量和尺寸的可变性，以及缺乏真实标注数据的挑战。

Method: 采用Transformer架构处理可变数量和尺寸的实体，并设计定制模拟器生成计算廉价且充足的合成数据进行训练。

Result: 在着丝粒定位任务上，该方法在多种基因组大小的物种中准确恢复了着丝粒的基因组位置。

Conclusion: 所提出的数据驱动方法有效利用了图间的共享结构（如局部模式的全局对齐），能够泛化到不同物种，为相互作用图推理提供了一种可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Inference from interaction maps, such as centromere identification from genome-wide chromosome conformation capture techniques -- notably Hi-C -- can be formulated as a generic inverse problem: infer a set of parameters given a map summarizing pairwise interactions between entities through blocks of variable numbers and sizes. In this work, we introduce a data-driven approach that leverages shared structure between these maps, such as global alignment between localized patterns, while handling the variability in number and size of entities arising in real-world data. Our approach relies on a transformer architecture capable of handling such variability and a custom simulator to generate abundant, yet computationally cheap synthetic data for training. Applied to the problem of centromere localization, the method accurately recovers their genomic positions across a wide range of species of various genome sizes.

</details>


### [73] [Alike Parts: A Feature-Informed Approach to Local and Global Prototype Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21646)
*Jacek Karolczak,Jerzy Stefanowski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种两层级特征重要性框架：局部层面通过"相似部件"方法高亮实例与原型共享的关键特征子集；全局层面在原型选择目标函数中加入特征重要性项以增强原型特征归因多样性。实验表明该方法在保持或提升预测保真度的同时不牺牲模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于原型的可解释性方法虽然提供直观的实例级解释，但缺乏特征级别的细粒度信息，难以精确指导用户关注哪些特征对分类决策最为关键。

Method: 1) 局部解释：提出"alike parts"方法，利用特征重要性分数突出分类实例与其最近原型之间最相关的共享特征子集。2) 全局解释：在原型选择目标函数中引入特征重要性项，主动促进所选原型的特征归因多样性。

Result: 在六个基准数据集上，增强的原型选择过程保持甚至在某些情况下提高了替代模型的预测保真度，表明特征多样性不会损害模型保真度。

Conclusion: 所提出的两层级特征重要性框架能够在保持模型预测性能的前提下，提供更具细粒度的原型解释，实现特征多样性与保真度的良好平衡。

Abstract: Prototype-based explanations offer an intuitive, example-based approach to support the interpretability of machine learning black box classifiers but often lack feature-level granularity. We introduce a framework that integrates feature importance at two levels to address this gap. First, for local explanations, we propose \textit{alike parts}: a method that uses feature importance scores to highlight the most relevant, shared feature subsets between a classified instance and its nearest prototype, guiding user attention. Second, we augment the global prototype selection objective function with a feature importance term to actively promote diversity in the feature attributions of the selected prototypes. Experiments on six benchmark datasets show that this augmented selection process maintains or, in some cases, increases the prediction fidelity of the surrogate model, suggesting that feature diversity does not compromise model fidelity.

</details>


### [74] [EntmaxKV: Support-Aware Decoding for Entmax Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21649)
*Gonçalo Duarte,Miguel Couceiro,Marcos V. Treviso*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EntmaxKV是一种面向entmax注意力机制的稀疏解码框架，通过利用entmax产生的精确零值，在KV缓存加载前实现稀疏性，从而在长上下文解码中显著减少内存流量和延迟。相比基于softmax的稀疏解码方法，EntmaxKV能更准确地恢复entmax支持集，在匹配的KV预算下丢失更少的概率质量，并在1M上下文长度下实现最高3.36倍（softmax基线）和5.43倍（entmax基线）的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文解码受限于KV缓存内存流量，因为每个生成的token需要访问随上下文长度线性增长的缓存。现有基于softmax的稀疏解码方法会截断尾部概率质量导致误差，而entmax注意力能产生精确零值，但尚无针对其自回归解码瓶颈的稀疏化方案——在已知稀疏性之前，密集推理仍需流式传输完整KV缓存。

Method: EntmaxKV包含三个核心组件：查询感知的页面评分、支持感知的候选选择、稀疏entmax注意力。提出基于丢弃概率质量δ的截断误差分析框架，证明当entmax支持集被恢复时输出误差消失。进一步引入高斯感知的entmax选择器，通过轻量级页面统计估计entmax阈值，自适应调整选中的预算。

Result: 在匹配的KV预算下，EntmaxKV比基于softmax的稀疏解码丢弃更少的概率质量、保留更多的支持token、实现更低的输出误差。在长上下文和语言建模基准测试中，使用少量KV缓存即可接近全缓存entmax性能，在1M上下文长度下实现最高3.36倍（softmax基线）和5.43倍（entmax基线）的加速。

Conclusion: EntmaxKV是首个面向entmax的稀疏解码框架，通过将稀疏解码从softmax的密集尾部近似问题转化为entmax的支持集恢复问题，实现了更精确且高效的稀疏注意力解码，为长上下文推理提供了一种有效的加速方案。

Abstract: Long-context decoding is increasingly limited by KV-cache memory traffic since each generated token attends over a cache whose size grows linearly with context length. Existing sparse decoding methods reduce this cost by selecting subsets of tokens or pages, but are designed for softmax attention, whose dense tails make any truncation discard nonzero probability mass. In contrast, $α$-entmax produces exact zeros, turning sparse decoding from dense-tail approximation into support recovery: if the selected candidates contain the entmax support, sparse decoding remains exact. While recent entmax kernels enable efficient training, they do not address the autoregressive decoding bottleneck, where dense inference still streams the full KV cache before sparsity is known. In this work, we introduce EntmaxKV, an entmax-native sparse decoding framework that exploits sparsity before KV pages are loaded.
